# Word names

<!-- data-table: parsed by runtime/research/markdown_data.py; keep one table, one row per entry -->

Every alien word of the game table `nms_dialog_gcalienspeechtable` (4833 entries): the word identifier, the group that teaches it for each race (empty where the race does not have the word) and its text in the 14 interface languages, from the game's language files. 2151 words, 4829 word and race pairs, 3830 groups. Generated by `list-word-names.py`; do not edit by hand. Entries left out because the game gives them no race that has words: MINING (race None, group BUI_MINE). Words without a text in a language take the English one: pt-BR 0, pt-PT 0, ja-JP 0, en-US 0, fr-FR 0, it-IT 0, de-DE 0, es-ES 0, nl-NL 0, ko-KR 0, pl-PL 0, ru-RU 0, zh-CN 0, zh-TW 0.
